aawc_caravanserai_01 = {
 construction_time = 235
 cost = { gold = 135 piety = 0 prestige = 0 }
 type_icon = "icon_building_market_villages.dds"
 show_disabled = yes
 can_construct = {
  trigger_if = {
  limit = {
  has_holding_type = city_holding
  is_county_capital = no
  culture = {
  has_cultural_parameter = watermills_windmills_cities
  }
  }
  has_building_or_higher = city_01
  NOR = {
  OR = { has_building_or_higher = watermills_01 has_building_or_higher = aawc_watermills_01 }
  OR = { has_building_or_higher = windmills_01 has_building_or_higher = aawc_windmills_01 }
  }
  }
  trigger_else = {
  building_requirement_castle_city_church = { LEVEL = 01 }
  is_county_capital = yes
  }
 }
 can_construct_potential = {
  OR = { has_holding_type = castle_holding has_holding_type = tribal_holding has_holding_type = city_holding has_holding_type = church_holding has_holding_type = temple_citadel_holding has_holding_type = pirate_den_holding has_holding_type = settlement_holding }
  building_caravanserai_requirement_terrain = yes
  building_requirement_castle_city_church = { LEVEL = 01 }
 }
 can_construct_showing_failures_only = {
  building_requirement_tribal = no
  scope:holder.culture = {
  has_innovation = innovation_guilds
  }
  trigger_if = {
  limit = {
  culture = { has_cultural_parameter = second_caravanserai }
  }
  custom_tooltip = {
  text = has_two_caravanserai_in_county
  county = {
  any_county_province = {
  count < 2
  OR = { has_building_or_higher = caravanserai_01 has_building_or_higher = aawc_caravanserai_01 }
  }
  }
  }
  }
  trigger_else = {
  custom_tooltip = {
  text = not_allowed_second_caravanserai
  county = {
  any_county_province = {
  count < 1
  OR = { has_building_or_higher = caravanserai_01 has_building_or_higher = aawc_caravanserai_01 }
  }
  }
  }
  }
 }
 is_enabled = {
  trigger_if = {
  limit = {
  NOT = {
  culture = {
  has_cultural_parameter = watermills_windmills_cities
  }
  }
  }
  is_county_capital = yes
  }
  trigger_else = {
  OR = {
  is_county_capital = yes
  AND = {
  NOR = {
  OR = { has_building_or_higher = watermills_01 has_building_or_higher = aawc_watermills_01 }
  OR = { has_building_or_higher = windmills_01 has_building_or_higher = aawc_windmills_01 }
  }
  has_building_or_higher = city_01
  }
  }
  }
  trigger_if = {
  limit = {
  culture = { has_cultural_parameter = second_caravanserai }
  }
  county = {
  any_county_province = {
  count <= 2
  OR = { has_building_or_higher = caravanserai_01 has_building_or_higher = aawc_caravanserai_01 }
  }
  }
  }
  trigger_else = {
  county = {
  any_county_province = {
  count <= 1
  OR = { has_building_or_higher = caravanserai_01 has_building_or_higher = aawc_caravanserai_01 }
  }
  }
  }
 }
 next_building = aawc_caravanserai_02
 county_modifier = { development_growth_factor = 0 }
 effect_desc = aawc_stage3_aawc_caravanserai_01_effects

  }
  }

  }

  }

  OR = {

  AND = {
  OR = { has_building_or_higher = logging_camps_04 has_building_or_higher = aawc_logging_camps_04 }

  has_cultural_parameter = watermills_forestries_unlock
  }
  }
  }
 }

  }
